<!-- Micro-Interaction Script for Dynamic Slug and Form Elements -->
<script>
    function syncSubdomain(name) {
      if (!name) return;
      const cleanSlug = name.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '').slice(0, 14);
      const slugInput = document.getElementById('slugInput');
      if (slugInput) {
        slugInput.value = cleanSlug || 'lab';
        updateSchemaPreview(slugInput.value);
      }
    }

    function updateSchemaPreview(slug) {
      const formatted = 'tenant_' + (slug ? slug.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '') : 'custom');
      const tag = document.getElementById('schemaTag');
      if (tag) tag.innerText = formatted;
      const summary = document.getElementById('bottomSummary');
      if (summary) {
        summary.innerHTML = 'Will deploy isolated PostgreSQL schema <code class="font-code-sm font-bold text-primary">' + formatted + '</code> and dispatch onboarding link.';
      }
    }

    function regeneratePass() {
      const chars = 'ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789';
      let part1 = '';
      for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) part1 += chars.charAt(Math.floor(Math.random() * chars.length));
      let part2 = '';
      for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) part2 += chars.charAt(Math.floor(Math.random() * chars.length));
      const newPass = 'LBF-' + part1 + '-' + part2 + '#';
      document.getElementById('tempPassCode').innerText = newPass;
    }

    function adjustStaff(delta) {
      const input = document.getElementById('staffSeatsInput');
      let val = parseInt(input.value, 10) || 5;
      val = Math.max(1, Math.min(100, val + delta));
      input.value = val;
    }

    function setBranchCount(btn, count) {
      const pills = document.querySelectorAll('.branch-pill');
      pills.forEach(p => {
        p.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-lowest', 'text-primary', 'font-bold', 'shadow-xs');
        p.classList.add('text-on-surface-variant');
      });
      btn.classList.remove('text-on-surface-variant');
      btn.classList.add('bg-surface-container-lowest', 'text-primary', 'font-bold', 'shadow-xs');
    }

    // Plan radio card selection logic
    const planCards = document.querySelectorAll('.plan-card');
    planCards.forEach(card => {
      card.addEventListener('click', function() {
        planCards.forEach(c => {
          c.classList.remove('selected', 'bg-surface-container-high');
          c.classList.add('bg-surface-container-low');
          const check = c.querySelector('.check-icon');
          if (check) {
            check.classList.remove('bg-primary', 'text-on-primary');
            check.classList.add('bg-surface-container-highest');
            check.innerHTML = '';
          }
        });
        this.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-low');
        this.classList.add('selected', 'bg-surface-container-high');
        const check = this.querySelector('.check-icon');
        if (check) {
          check.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-highest');
          check.classList.add('bg-primary', 'text-on-primary');
          check.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[12px]">check</span>';
        }
      });
    });

    function simulateProvisioning() {
      const btn = document.getElementById('deployButton');
      const originalHTML = btn.innerHTML;
      btn.disabled = true;
      btn.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined animate-spin text-[18px]">progress_activity</span><span>Allocating Isolated Schema...</span>';
      
      setTimeout(() => {
        btn.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]">task_alt</span><span>Tenant Deployed & Verified</span>';
        btn.classList.remove('bg-primary');
        btn.classList.add('bg-primary-container');
        setTimeout(() => {
          btn.innerHTML = originalHTML;
          btn.disabled = false;
          btn.classList.remove('bg-primary-container');
          btn.classList.add('bg-primary');
        }, 3000);
      }, 1600);
    }
  </script>
</div></div></main></div></body></html>

  (function initLabsDirectory() {
    const searchInput = document.getElementById('lab-search-input');
    const statusSelect = document.getElementById('filter-status');
    const regionSelect = document.getElementById('filter-region');
    const planSelect = document.getElementById('filter-plan');
    const resetBtn = document.getElementById('filter-reset-btn');
    const rows = document.querySelectorAll('.lab-row');
    const checkAll = document.getElementById('check-all');
    const rowCheckboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.row-checkbox');
    const paginationInfo = document.getElementById('pagination-info');

    const modal = document.getElementById('provision-modal');
    const openBtn = document.getElementById('btn-provision-open');
    const closeBtn = document.getElementById('btn-provision-close');
    const cancelBtn = document.getElementById('btn-provision-cancel');
    const form = document.getElementById('provision-form');

    function applyFilters() {
      const term = (searchInput?.value || '').toLowerCase().trim();
      const status = statusSelect?.value || 'ALL';
      const region = regionSelect?.value || 'ALL';
      const plan = planSelect?.value || 'ALL';

      let visibleCount = 0;

      rows.forEach(row => {
        const text = row.innerText.toLowerCase();
        const rowStatus = row.getAttribute('data-status');
        const rowRegion = row.getAttribute('data-region');
        const rowPlan = row.getAttribute('data-plan');

        const matchesTerm = !term || text.includes(term);
        const matchesStatus = status === 'ALL' || rowStatus === status;
        const matchesRegion = region === 'ALL' || rowRegion === region;
        const matchesPlan = plan === 'ALL' || rowPlan === plan;

        if (matchesTerm && matchesStatus && matchesRegion && matchesPlan) {
          row.style.display = '';
          visibleCount++;
        } else {
          row.style.display = 'none';
        }
      });

      if (paginationInfo) {
        paginationInfo.textContent = `Showing 1-${visibleCount} of 12 labs`;
      }
    }

    searchInput?.addEventListener('input', applyFilters);
    statusSelect?.addEventListener('change', applyFilters);
    regionSelect?.addEventListener('change', applyFilters);
    planSelect?.addEventListener('change', applyFilters);

    resetBtn?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      if (searchInput) searchInput.value = '';
      if (statusSelect) statusSelect.value = 'ALL';
      if (regionSelect) regionSelect.value = 'ALL';
      if (planSelect) planSelect.value = 'ALL';
      applyFilters();
    });

    checkAll?.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
      const checked = e.target.checked;
      rowCheckboxes.forEach(cb => {
        cb.checked = checked;
      });
    });

    openBtn?.addEventListener('click', () => {
      modal?.classList.remove('hidden');
      modal?.classList.add('flex');
    });

    function closeModal() {
      modal?.classList.add('hidden');
      modal?.classList.remove('flex');
    }

    closeBtn?.addEventListener('click', closeModal);
    cancelBtn?.addEventListener('click', closeModal);

    form?.addEventListener('submit', (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      closeModal();
      alert('Provisioning initiated! Isolated tenant workspace configured.');
    });
  })();
</script></main></div>

</body></html>

<!-- Interactive DOM Wiring Script -->
<script>
  function syncPreview(name) {
    const previewEl = document.getElementById('previewLabName');
    if (previewEl) {
      previewEl.innerText = name.trim() || 'New Diagnostic Tenant';
    }
  }

  function syncSubdomain(val) {
    const previewSub = document.getElementById('previewSubdomain');
    const clean = val.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9-]/g, '');
    if (previewSub) {
      previewSub.innerText = (clean || 'instance') + '.labflow.pk';
    }
  }

  function syncOwnerName(name) {
    const preview = document.getElementById('previewOwner');
    if (preview) {
      preview.innerText = name.trim() || 'Pending Assignment';
    }
  }

  function syncEmail(email) {
    const preview = document.getElementById('previewEmail');
    if (preview) {
      preview.innerText = email.trim() || 'pending@dispatch.pk';
    }
  }

  function syncBranchQuota(val) {
    const preview = document.getElementById('previewBranches');
    if (preview) {
      preview.innerText = val === 'Unlimited' ? 'Unlimited Branches' : val + ' Centers';
    }
  }

  function syncStaffQuota(val) {
    const preview = document.getElementById('previewStaff');
    if (preview) {
      preview.innerText = (val || 0) + ' Logins';
    }
  }

  function incrementStaff() {
    const input = document.getElementById('maxStaffLogins');
    let val = parseInt(input.value, 10) || 0;
    val += 1;
    input.value = val;
    syncStaffQuota(val);
  }

  function decrementStaff() {
    const input = document.getElementById('maxStaffLogins');
    let val = parseInt(input.value, 10) || 0;
    if (val > 1) {
      val -= 1;
      input.value = val;
      syncStaffQuota(val);
    }
  }

  function selectPlan(tier, price, details) {
    const cards = document.querySelectorAll('.plan-card');
    cards.forEach(card => {
      card.classList.remove('border-2', 'border-primary', 'bg-surface-container-lowest', 'ring-1', 'ring-primary/20', 'shadow-sm');
      card.classList.add('border-outline-variant/60', 'bg-surface-container-low');
    });

    const eventSource = event.currentTarget;
    if (eventSource) {
      eventSource.classList.remove('border-outline-variant/60', 'bg-surface-container-low');
      eventSource.classList.add('border-2', 'border-primary', 'bg-surface-container-lowest', 'ring-1', 'ring-primary/20', 'shadow-sm');
      const radio = eventSource.querySelector('input[type="radio"]');
      if (radio) radio.checked = true;
    }

    const preview = document.getElementById('previewPlan');
    if (preview) {
      const tierTitle = tier.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + tier.slice(1);
      preview.innerText = tierTitle + ' (' + price + ')';
    }
  }

  function toggleRole(roleName) {
    // Micro interaction feedback
  }

  function submitProvisioning() {
    const submitBtn = document.getElementById('btnSubmit');
    const toast = document.getElementById('toastNotification');
    const toastMsg = document.getElementById('toastMessage');
    const labName = document.getElementById('labLegalName').value || 'Tenant';
    const subdomain = document.getElementById('subdomainPrefix').value || 'node';

    if (submitBtn) {
      submitBtn.disabled = true;
      submitBtn.innerHTML = `
        <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px] animate-spin">progress_activity</span>
        <span>Provisioning Tenant Cloud...</span>
      `;
    }

    setTimeout(() => {
      if (submitBtn) {
        submitBtn.disabled = false;
        submitBtn.innerHTML = `
          <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]">done_all</span>
          <span>Access Granted!</span>
        `;
        submitBtn.classList.remove('bg-primary');
        submitBtn.classList.add('bg-primary-container');
      }

      if (toast && toastMsg) {
        toastMsg.innerText = labName + ' deployed to ' + subdomain + '.labflow.pk. Temporary keys dispatched.';
        toast.classList.remove('translate-y-32', 'opacity-0');
        toast.classList.add('translate-y-0', 'opacity-100');

        setTimeout(() => {
          toast.classList.remove('translate-y-0', 'opacity-100');
          toast.classList.add('translate-y-32', 'opacity-0');
        }, 5000);
      }
    }, 1400);
  }
</script></main></div>

</body></html>

<!-- Interactive DOM Logic for Live Filter & Select States -->
<script>
  // DOM element handles
  const searchInput = document.getElementById('tableSearchInput');
  const filterStatus = document.getElementById('filterStatus');
  const filterPlan = document.getElementById('filterPlan');
  const filterCity = document.getElementById('filterCity');
  const labsTableBody = document.getElementById('labsTableBody');
  const filteredResultsLabel = document.getElementById('filteredResultsLabel');
  const selectAllCheckbox = document.getElementById('selectAllCheckbox');
  const selectedRowCount = document.getElementById('selectedRowCount');
  const provisionModal = document.getElementById('provisionModal');
  const btnProvisionModal = document.getElementById('btnProvisionModal');

  // Trigger filters
  function filterTable() {
    const query = searchInput.value.toLowerCase().trim();
    const status = filterStatus.value.toLowerCase();
    const plan = filterPlan.value.toLowerCase();
    const city = filterCity.value.toLowerCase();

    const rows = labsTableBody.querySelectorAll('tr');
    let visibleCount = 0;

    rows.forEach(row => {
      const rowCity = (row.getAttribute('data-city') || '').toLowerCase();
      const rowStatus = (row.getAttribute('data-status') || '').toLowerCase();
      const rowPlan = (row.getAttribute('data-plan') || '').toLowerCase();
      const rowText = row.innerText.toLowerCase();

      const matchesQuery = query === '' || rowText.includes(query);
      const matchesStatus = status === 'all' || rowStatus === status;
      const matchesPlan = plan === 'all' || rowPlan === plan;
      const matchesCity = city === 'all' || rowCity === city;

      if (matchesQuery && matchesStatus && matchesPlan && matchesCity) {
        row.style.display = '';
        visibleCount++;
      } else {
        row.style.display = 'none';
      }
    });

    filteredResultsLabel.textContent = `Showing ${visibleCount} of 8 tenant labs`;
  }

  // Event Listeners for Filters
  searchInput.addEventListener('input', filterTable);
  filterStatus.addEventListener('change', filterTable);
  filterPlan.addEventListener('change', filterTable);
  filterCity.addEventListener('change', filterTable);

  function resetFilters() {
    searchInput.value = '';
    filterStatus.value = 'all';
    filterPlan.value = 'all';
    filterCity.value = 'all';
    filterTable();
  }

  // Row selection logic
  function updateSelectedCount() {
    const checkboxes = labsTableBody.querySelectorAll('.row-checkbox:checked');
    selectedRowCount.textContent = checkboxes.length;
    selectAllCheckbox.checked = (checkboxes.length === labsTableBody.querySelectorAll('.row-checkbox').length && checkboxes.length > 0);
  }

  selectAllCheckbox.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
    const checkboxes = labsTableBody.querySelectorAll('.row-checkbox');
    checkboxes.forEach(cb => {
      if (cb.closest('tr').style.display !== 'none') {
        cb.checked = e.target.checked;
      }
    });
    updateSelectedCount();
  });

  labsTableBody.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
    if (e.target.classList.contains('row-checkbox')) {
      updateSelectedCount();
    }
  });

  // Modal interactions
  btnProvisionModal.addEventListener('click', () => {
    provisionModal.classList.remove('hidden');
  });

  function closeProvisionModal() {
    provisionModal.classList.add('hidden');
  }

  function simulateProvision() {
    closeProvisionModal();
    alert('Tenant Provisioning Pipeline Initialized. Schema creation job queued to Kafka Broker.');
  }

  // CSV exporter simulation
  function exportCSV() {
    const rows = labsTableBody.querySelectorAll('tr');
    let csvContent = "data:text/csv;charset=utf-8,Lab Name,Schema ID,Owner,Phone,Plan,Status,Branches,Staff,MRR (PKR),Joined\n";
    rows.forEach(r => {
      if (r.style.display !== 'none') {
        const textData = Array.from(r.querySelectorAll('td')).map(td => `"${td.innerText.replace(/\n/g, ' ').replace(/"/g, '""').trim()}"`);
        csvContent += textData.join(",") + "\n";
      }
    });
    const encodedUri = encodeURI(csvContent);
    const link = document.createElement("a");
    link.setAttribute("href", encodedUri);
    link.setAttribute("download", "labflow_tenants_snapshot.csv");
    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    document.body.removeChild(link);
  }
</script></div></main></div></body></html>

<script>
  // Quick Search Filtering for Table
  const userSearch = document.getElementById('userQuickSearch');
  const tableRows = document.querySelectorAll('#labOwnersTableBody tr');

  if (userSearch) {
    userSearch.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
      const query = e.target.value.toLowerCase().trim();
      tableRows.forEach(row => {
        const text = row.innerText.toLowerCase();
        row.style.display = text.includes(query) ? '' : 'none';
      });
    });
  }

  // Filter Buttons Interaction
  const filterButtons = document.querySelectorAll('#filterButtonGroup button');
  filterButtons.forEach(btn => {
    btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      filterButtons.forEach(b => {
        b.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-lowest', 'text-on-surface', 'shadow-sm');
        b.classList.add('text-on-surface-variant');
      });
      btn.classList.add('bg-surface-container-lowest', 'text-on-surface', 'shadow-sm');
      btn.classList.remove('text-on-surface-variant');

      const filterVal = btn.dataset.filter;
      tableRows.forEach(row => {
        if (filterVal === 'all') {
          row.style.display = '';
        } else if (filterVal === 'active') {
          row.style.display = row.innerText.includes('ACTIVE') ? '' : 'none';
        } else if (filterVal === 'suspended') {
          row.style.display = row.innerText.includes('SUSPENDED') ? '' : 'none';
        } else if (filterVal === 'internal') {
          row.style.display = 'none'; // Internal operators are displayed below in the Platform team section
        }
      });
    });
  });

  // Table Bulk Selection
  const selectAll = document.getElementById('selectAllLabOwners');
  const rowCheckboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.tenant-row-check');
  const selectedCountText = document.getElementById('selectedCountText');

  if (selectAll) {
    selectAll.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
      rowCheckboxes.forEach(cb => cb.checked = e.target.checked);
      updateSelectedCount();
    });
  }

  rowCheckboxes.forEach(cb => {
    cb.addEventListener('change', updateSelectedCount);
  });

  function updateSelectedCount() {
    const checked = Array.from(rowCheckboxes).filter(cb => cb.checked).length;
    if (selectedCountText) {
      selectedCountText.innerText = `${checked} selected`;
    }
  }

  // Invite Modal Logic
  const inviteBtn = document.getElementById('btnInviteMember');
  const inviteModal = document.getElementById('inviteModal');
  const closeInviteModal = document.getElementById('closeInviteModal');
  const cancelInviteModal = document.getElementById('cancelInviteModal');
  const confirmInviteSubmit = document.getElementById('confirmInviteSubmit');

  function toggleModal(show) {
    if (inviteModal) {
      if (show) {
        inviteModal.classList.remove('hidden');
        inviteModal.classList.add('flex');
      } else {
        inviteModal.classList.add('hidden');
        inviteModal.classList.remove('flex');
      }
    }
  }

  if (inviteBtn) inviteBtn.addEventListener('click', () => toggleModal(true));
  if (closeInviteModal) closeInviteModal.addEventListener('click', () => toggleModal(false));
  if (cancelInviteModal) cancelInviteModal.addEventListener('click', () => toggleModal(false));
  if (confirmInviteSubmit) {
    confirmInviteSubmit.addEventListener('click', () => {
      toggleModal(false);
      alert('Verification token dispatched successfully to Pakistan operator node.');
    });
  }
</script></main></div>

</body></html>

<!-- Interactive Client-side Script for Filtering & Row Toggles -->
<script>
    (function() {
      const searchInput = document.getElementById('searchInput');
      const statusFilter = document.getElementById('statusFilter');
      const planFilter = document.getElementById('planFilter');
      const cycleFilter = document.getElementById('cycleFilter');
      const quickPills = document.querySelectorAll('.quick-pill');
      const tableRows = document.querySelectorAll('.table-row-item');
      const selectAll = document.getElementById('selectAllCheckbox');
      const rowCheckboxes = document.querySelectorAll('.row-checkbox');
      const reminderBtn = document.getElementById('whatsappReminderBtn');

      let currentQuickFilter = 'ALL';

      function applyFilters() {
        const query = (searchInput.value || '').toLowerCase().trim();
        const selectedStatus = statusFilter.value;
        const selectedPlan = planFilter.value;
        const selectedCycle = cycleFilter.value;

        tableRows.forEach(row => {
          const rowText = row.innerText.toLowerCase();
          const rowStatus = row.getAttribute('data-status');
          const rowPlan = row.getAttribute('data-plan');
          const rowCycle = row.getAttribute('data-cycle');
          const isDueWeek = row.getAttribute('data-due') === 'week';

          let matchesSearch = !query || rowText.includes(query);
          let matchesStatus = (selectedStatus === 'ALL') || (rowStatus === selectedStatus);
          let matchesPlan = (selectedPlan === 'ALL') || (rowPlan === selectedPlan);
          let matchesCycle = (selectedCycle === 'ALL') || (rowCycle && rowCycle.includes(selectedCycle));

          let matchesQuick = true;
          if (currentQuickFilter === 'OVERDUE') {
            matchesQuick = rowStatus === 'OVERDUE';
          } else if (currentQuickFilter === 'DUE_WEEK') {
            matchesQuick = isDueWeek;
          }

          if (matchesSearch && matchesStatus && matchesPlan && matchesCycle && matchesQuick) {
            row.style.display = '';
          } else {
            row.style.display = 'none';
          }
        });
      }

      if (searchInput) searchInput.addEventListener('input', applyFilters);
      if (statusFilter) statusFilter.addEventListener('change', applyFilters);
      if (planFilter) planFilter.addEventListener('change', applyFilters);
      if (cycleFilter) cycleFilter.addEventListener('change', applyFilters);

      quickPills.forEach(pill => {
        pill.addEventListener('click', function() {
          quickPills.forEach(p => p.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-highest', 'font-bold'));
          this.classList.add('bg-surface-container-highest', 'font-bold');
          currentQuickFilter = this.getAttribute('data-filter');
          applyFilters();
        });
      });

      if (selectAll) {
        selectAll.addEventListener('change', function() {
          rowCheckboxes.forEach(cb => {
            if (cb.closest('tr').style.display !== 'none') {
              cb.checked = selectAll.checked;
            }
          });
        });
      }

      if (reminderBtn) {
        reminderBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
          alert('Automated payment reminder dispatch queued for 2 delinquent tenants via Pakistan Telecommunication Authority approved SMS & WhatsApp Business API gateway.');
        });
      }
    })();
  </script>
</div></main></div>

<script>
    function showNotification(text) {
      const toast = document.getElementById('toastMessage');
      const toastText = document.getElementById('toastText');
      if (toast && toastText) {
        toastText.textContent = text;
        toast.classList.remove('hidden');
        setTimeout(() => {
          toast.classList.add('hidden');
        }, 5000);
      }
    }

    function dismissToast() {
      const toast = document.getElementById('toastMessage');
      if (toast) toast.classList.add('hidden');
    }

    function handleManualPayment(e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      const rail = document.getElementById('paymentRail').value;
      const ref = document.getElementById('transactionRef').value;
      const date = document.getElementById('settlementDate').value;
      const amount = document.getElementById('settlementAmount').value;

      // Update the table first row to PAID
      const statusBadge = document.getElementById('rowStatusOverdue');
      const payBtn = document.getElementById('btnRowPayNow');
      if (statusBadge) {
        statusBadge.textContent = 'PAID';
        statusBadge.className = 'inline-flex items-center px-space-xs py-space-2xs rounded bg-surface-container-high text-primary font-code-sm text-code-sm uppercase font-bold';
      }
      if (payBtn) {
        payBtn.outerHTML = '<button onclick="downloadReceipt(\'LF-2025-0892\')" class="h-7 px-space-sm bg-surface-container hover:bg-surface-container-high text-on-surface transition-colors rounded font-label-sm text-label-sm inline-flex items-center gap-space-2xs shadow-sm"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[14px]">download</span>PDF Receipt</button>';
      }

      showNotification('Settlement recorded! Ref ' + ref + ' verified. Tenant overdue status cleared in node cache.');
    }

    function extendGracePeriod() {
      showNotification('Grace period extended by 7 days until April 05, 2025 for City Diagnostic Lab.');
    }

    function onTierSelect(radio) {
      const btn = document.getElementById('btnApplyTier');
      if (!btn) return;
      if (radio.value !== 'growth') {
        btn.disabled = false;
        btn.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-high', 'text-outline', 'cursor-not-allowed');
        btn.classList.add('bg-primary', 'text-on-primary', 'hover:bg-primary-container', 'shadow-sm');
      } else {
        btn.disabled = true;
        btn.classList.remove('bg-primary', 'text-on-primary', 'hover:bg-primary-container', 'shadow-sm');
        btn.classList.add('bg-surface-container-high', 'text-outline', 'cursor-not-allowed');
      }
    }

    function applyTierMigration() {
      const selected = document.querySelector('input[name="licensingTier"]:checked');
      if (selected) {
        showNotification('License tier change to "' + selected.value.toUpperCase() + '" staged. Dispatching tenant config update.');
      }
    }

    function scrollToPay() {
      const el = document.getElementById('settlementForm');
      if (el) {
        el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
        document.getElementById('transactionRef').focus();
      }
    }

    function triggerWhatsAppModal() {
      const modal = document.getElementById('modalWhatsapp');
      if (modal) modal.classList.remove('hidden');
    }

    function closeWhatsAppModal() {
      const modal = document.getElementById('modalWhatsapp');
      if (modal) modal.classList.add('hidden');
    }

    function sendWhatsAppNotification() {
      closeWhatsAppModal();
      showNotification('WhatsApp notification dispatched via Infobip gateway to Dr. Tariq (+92 300 8472910).');
    }

    function downloadTaxInvoice() {
      showNotification('Downloading PRA-certified tax invoice #LF-2025-0892 (PDF)...');
    }

    function downloadReceipt(invoiceId) {
      showNotification('Downloading official LabFlow settlement receipt for #' + invoiceId + '...');
    }

    function exportLedgerCSV() {
      showNotification('Exporting CDL-0941 audit ledger as CSV format.');
    }

    function confirmSuspendLab() {
      if (confirm('CRITICAL ACTION: Are you sure you want to suspend analyzer instrument syncing and access for City Diagnostic Lab (LAB-LHR-0941)?')) {
        showNotification('Tenant City Diagnostic Lab access suspended. Alert sent to director.');
      }
    }
  </script>

  // Simple micro-interaction for incident table
  document.getElementById('autoRetryAllBtn')?.addEventListener('click', function() {
    const btn = this;
    const originalText = btn.innerHTML;
    btn.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] animate-spin">refresh</span> Dispatching...';
    btn.disabled = true;
    setTimeout(() => {
      btn.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px]">check</span> Queued (4)';
      setTimeout(() => {
        btn.innerHTML = originalText;
        btn.disabled = false;
      }, 2000);
    }, 1200);
  });

  document.getElementById('clearResolvedBtn')?.addEventListener('click', function() {
    const rows = document.querySelectorAll('#incidentsTableBody tr');
    if (rows.length > 2) {
      rows[rows.length - 1].remove();
    }
  });

<div class="flex items-center gap-space-sm bg-surface-container-lowest px-space-md py-space-xs rounded shadow-sm self-start md:self-auto">

<div class="bg-inverse-surface text-inverse-on-surface px-space-md py-space-sm rounded shadow-lg flex items-center gap-space-sm">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-primary-fixed text-[20px]">task_alt</span>

<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm font-bold" id="toast-title">Settings Saved Successfully</span>

<script>
    (function() {
      // Toast notification helper
      const toast = document.getElementById('toast');
      const toastTitle = document.getElementById('toast-title');
      const toastDesc = document.getElementById('toast-desc');
      let toastTimer = null;

      function showToast(title, desc) {
        if (!toast) return;
        toastTitle.textContent = title;
        toastDesc.textContent = desc;
        toast.classList.remove('translate-y-16', 'opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        toast.classList.add('translate-y-0', 'opacity-100');
        clearTimeout(toastTimer);
        toastTimer = setTimeout(() => {
          toast.classList.remove('translate-y-0', 'opacity-100');
          toast.classList.add('translate-y-16', 'opacity-0', 'pointer-events-none');
        }, 3200);
      }

      // Save Pricing Action
      const btnPricing = document.getElementById('btn-save-pricing');
      if (btnPricing) {
        btnPricing.addEventListener('click', () => {
          btnPricing.disabled = true;
          const origText = btnPricing.innerHTML;
          btnPricing.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] animate-spin">refresh</span><span>Saving...</span>';
          setTimeout(() => {
            btnPricing.disabled = false;
            btnPricing.innerHTML = origText;
            showToast('Pricing Matrix Persisted', 'Updated PKR rates broadcasted to 12 active billing agents.');
          }, 600);
        });
      }

      // Founding Offer Action
      const btnFounding = document.getElementById('btn-founding-update');
      if (btnFounding) {
        btnFounding.addEventListener('click', () => {
          btnFounding.disabled = true;
          const origText = btnFounding.innerHTML;
          btnFounding.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] animate-spin">refresh</span><span>Updating...</span>';
          setTimeout(() => {
            btnFounding.disabled = false;
            btnFounding.innerHTML = origText;
            showToast('Founding Campaign Updated', 'Reserved slots and promotional criteria synchronized.');
          }, 500);
        });
      }

      // Save Governance Action
      const btnGov = document.getElementById('btn-save-governance');
      if (btnGov) {
        btnGov.addEventListener('click', () => {
          btnGov.disabled = true;
          const origText = btnGov.innerHTML;
          btnGov.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px] animate-spin">refresh</span><span>Saving...</span>';
          setTimeout(() => {
            btnGov.disabled = false;
            btnGov.innerHTML = origText;
            showToast('Governance Settings Applied', 'Cluster routing parameters flushed and active.');
          }, 700);
        });
      }

      // Toggle Buttons Interactive state handler
      document.querySelectorAll('.gov-toggle, #toggle-founding').forEach(toggleBtn => {
        toggleBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
          const thumb = this.querySelector('span');
          const isCurrentlyActive = this.classList.contains('bg-primary-container');
          
          if (isCurrentlyActive) {
            this.classList.remove('bg-primary-container');
            this.classList.add('bg-surface-container-highest');
            if (thumb) {
              thumb.classList.remove('translate-x-5');
              thumb.classList.add('translate-x-0.5');
            }
            this.setAttribute('data-state', 'false');
          } else {
            this.classList.remove('bg-surface-container-highest');
            this.classList.add('bg-primary-container');
            if (thumb) {
              thumb.classList.remove('translate-x-0.5');
              thumb.classList.add('translate-x-5');
            }
            this.setAttribute('data-state', 'true');
          }
        });
      });
    })();
  </script>
</div></main></div>

<script>
  // Simple interactive filter implementation
  (function() {
    const searchInput = document.getElementById('leadSearchInput');
    const statusSelect = document.getElementById('statusFilter');
    const citySelect = document.getElementById('cityFilter');
    const exportBtn = document.getElementById('exportCsvBtn');
    const table = document.getElementById('leadsTable');
    if (!table) return;
    const rows = table.querySelectorAll('tbody tr');

    function filterRows() {
      const query = (searchInput ? searchInput.value : '').toLowerCase().trim();
      const selectedStatus = statusSelect ? statusSelect.value : 'ALL';
      const selectedCity = citySelect ? citySelect.value : 'ALL';

      rows.forEach(row => {
        const text = row.innerText.toLowerCase();
        const rowStatus = row.getAttribute('data-status') || '';
        const rowCity = row.getAttribute('data-city') || '';

        const matchesQuery = query === '' || text.includes(query);
        const matchesStatus = selectedStatus === 'ALL' || rowStatus === selectedStatus;
        const matchesCity = selectedCity === 'ALL' || rowCity === selectedCity;

        if (matchesQuery && matchesStatus && matchesCity) {
          row.style.display = '';
        } else {
          row.style.display = 'none';
        }
      });
    }

    if (searchInput) searchInput.addEventListener('input', filterRows);
    if (statusSelect) statusSelect.addEventListener('change', filterRows);
    if (citySelect) citySelect.addEventListener('change', filterRows);

    if (exportBtn) {
      exportBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
        const csvContent = "data:text/csv;charset=utf-8," 
          + "Lab Name,City,Contact,Mobile,Tests/Day,Status\n"
          + "Bilal Diagnostic Centre,Gujranwala,Dr. Bilal Arshad,+923018472910,180,NEW\n"
          + "Horizon Clinical Pathology,Faisalabad,Dr. Farhan Saeed,+923214458902,350,CONTACTED\n"
          + "Al-Khidmat Diagnostic Clinic,Multan,Muhammad Zubair,+923337192841,90,DEMO DONE\n"
          + "Jinnah Specimen Collection & Lab,Sialkot,Dr. Rabia Qasim,+923459012344,220,DEMO DONE\n"
          + "Frontier Medical Diagnostic,Peshawar,Usman Khattak,+923005519823,500,CONVERTED\n"
          + "Rawal Care Lab & Imaging,Rawalpindi,Dr. Tahir Mehmood,+923126049281,60,CONVERTED\n"
          + "Indus Pathology Associates,Hyderabad,Dr. Asadullah Sheikh,+923228847190,120,LOST";
        
        const encodedUri = encodeURI(csvContent);
        const link = document.createElement("a");
        link.setAttribute("href", encodedUri);
        link.setAttribute("download", "labflow_demo_requests.csv");
        document.body.appendChild(link);
        link.click();
        document.body.removeChild(link);
      });
    }
  })();
</script></main></div>

<!-- Interactive JavaScript logic for micro-interactions -->
<script>
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
      // Notes persistence simulated feedback
      const saveBtn = document.getElementById('save-notes-btn');
      if (saveBtn) {
        saveBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          const originalText = saveBtn.innerHTML;
          saveBtn.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[15px]">check</span><span>Notes Saved</span>';
          saveBtn.classList.remove('bg-inverse-surface');
          saveBtn.classList.add('bg-primary-container');
          setTimeout(() => {
            saveBtn.innerHTML = originalText;
            saveBtn.classList.remove('bg-primary-container');
            saveBtn.classList.add('bg-inverse-surface');
          }, 2000);
        });
      }

      // Convert to Lab button interaction
      const convertBtn = document.getElementById('convert-lab-btn');
      if (convertBtn) {
        convertBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          const confirmed = confirm("Initialize tenant provisioning wizard for Bilal Diagnostic Centre with subdomain 'bilal-diag.labflow.pk'?");
          if (confirmed) {
            window.location.hash = "#labs/provision?lead=LEAD-2025-084";
          }
        });
      }

      // Timeline quick entry addition
      const addTimelineBtn = document.getElementById('add-timeline-btn');
      const logTextInput = document.getElementById('log-text-input');
      const logTypeSelect = document.getElementById('log-type-select');
      const timelineStream = document.getElementById('timeline-stream');

      if (addTimelineBtn && logTextInput && timelineStream) {
        addTimelineBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          const val = logTextInput.value.trim();
          if (!val) return;

          const type = logTypeSelect.value;
          let icon = 'phone';
          let title = 'Call Logged';
          if (type === 'whatsapp') { icon = 'chat'; title = 'WhatsApp Interaction'; }
          if (type === 'demo') { icon = 'slideshow'; title = 'Demo Conducted'; }
          if (type === 'note') { icon = 'note'; title = 'Operator Note'; }

          const item = document.createElement('div');
          item.className = 'relative group';
          item.innerHTML = `
            <div class="absolute -left-6 top-0.5 w-4 h-4 rounded-full bg-primary-container text-on-primary flex items-center justify-center">
              <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[10px]">${icon}</span>
            </div>
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between text-outline font-code-sm text-code-sm leading-none mb-1">
              <span class="font-semibold text-on-surface">${title}</span>
              <span>Just now</span>
            </div>
            <p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface-variant bg-surface p-space-xs rounded">
              ${val} &mdash; <span class="text-outline">Logged by Kamran</span>
            </p>
          `;

          timelineStream.insertBefore(item, timelineStream.firstChild);
          logTextInput.value = '';
        });
      }
    });
  </script>
</div></main></div>

</body></html>
